			}

		limit = {
				original_tag = COM
				date > 1999.04.13
				date < 1999.05.13
			}
		country_event = comoros.4
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
				original_tag = COM
				date > 2002.05.26
				date < 2002.06.26
			}
		country_event = comoros.5
	}
}
